Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in newtonian theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 373 and the invariant I(A,B) = 751 mod 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the newtonian decomposition. By minimality, the element a^373 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 751-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
